## False Positive Analysis
These are healthy subjects the model flagged as compensated.
Likely causes:
- Low-quality trial with noisy skeleton tracking
- Subject performing an unusual (but healthy) movement variant
- Insufficient training data for this subject's exercise style

## False Negative Analysis
These are compensated movements the model failed to detect.
Likely causes:
- Subtle compensatory pattern not well represented in training data
- Compensation occurring at joints not well attended by the model
- Short sequence with insufficient temporal context
